                    [32m'        const client = getSyncedStateClient(resolvedUrl);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        const [value, setValue] = useState(initialValue);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        const valueRef = useRef(value);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        valueRef.current = value;\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        const setSyncValue = useCallback((nextValue) => {\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            const resolved = typeof nextValue === "function"\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                ? nextValue(valueRef.current)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                : nextValue;\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            setValue(resolved);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            valueRef.current = resolved;\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            void client.setState(resolved, key);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        }, [client, key, setValue, valueRef]);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        useEffect(() => {\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            let isActive = true;\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            const handleUpdate = (next) => {\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                if (isActive) {\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    setValue(next);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    valueRef.current = next;\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                }\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            };\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            void client.getState(key).then((existing) => {\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                if (existing !== undefined && isActive) {\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    setValue(existing);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                    valueRef.current = existing;\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                }\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            });\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            void client.subscribe(key, handleUpdate);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            const unsubscribeStatus = options.onStatusChange\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                ? onStatusChange(resolvedUrl, options.onStatusChange)\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                : undefined;\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            return () => {\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                isActive = false;\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                unsubscribeStatus?.();\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                // Call unsubscribe when component unmounts\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                // Page reloads are handled by the beforeunload event listener in client-core.ts\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                void client.unsubscribe(key, handleUpdate).catch((error) => {\n'[39m +
                    [32m"                    // Log but don't throw - cleanup should not prevent unmounting\n"[39m +
                    [32m'                    console.error("[useSyncedState] Error during unsubscribe:", error);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                });\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            };\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        }, [client, key, setValue, valueRef]);\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return [value, setSyncValue];\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    };\n'[39m +
                    [32m'};\n'[39m +
                    [32m'export const useSyncedState = createSyncedStateHook();\n'[39m +
                    [32m''[39m,
                  isTruncated: [33mfalse[39m

Page</>\\n})\\n\\nroute(\\"/about\\", function handler() {\\n  return <>About Page</>\\n})\\n\\nroute(\\"/contact\\", function handler() {\\n  return <>Contact Page</>\\n})\\n```\\n\\n#### Dynamic \\"rwsdk/router\\";\\n\\nroute(\\"/api/status\\", function handler() {\\n  return new Response(\\"OK\\", {\\n    status: 200,\\n    headers: { \\"Content-Type\\": \\"text/plain\\" }\\n  })\\n})\\n```\\n\\n#### JSON Response\\n\\n```tsx\\nimport { route } from \\"rwsdk/router\\";\\n\\nroute(\\"/api/users/:id\\", function handler({ params }) {\\n  const userData = { id: params.id, })\\n})\\n```\\n\\n#### JSX/React Components Response\\n\\n```tsx\\nimport { route } from \\"rwsdk/router\\";\\nimport { UserProfile } from \'@/app/components/UserProfile\'\\n\\nroute(\\"/users/:id\\", function handler({ params }) {\\n  return <UserProfile userId={params.id} />\\n})\\n```\\n\\n#### Custom Document route(\'/post/:postId\', BlogPostPage),\\n  route(\'/post/:postId/edit\', [isAdminUser, BlogAdminPage])\\n]\\n```\\n\\nThen import these routes in your main worker file:\\n\\n```tsx\\n//             [32m'# Committing changes with git\n'[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +

            [32m'Git Safety Protocol:\n'[39m +

            [32m"- NEVER run destructive git commands (push --force, reset --hard, checkout ., restore ., clean -f, branch -D) unless the user explicitly requests these actions.             [32m'- NEVER skip hooks (--no-verify, --no-gpg-sign, etc) unless the user explicitly requests it\n'[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER run force push to main/master, warn the user if they request it\n'[39m +
            [32m'- CRITICAL: Always create NEW commits rather than amending, unless the user explicitly requests a git amend. Instead, after hook failure, fix the issue, re-stage, and create a NEW commit\n'[39m +
            [32m'- When staging files, prefer adding specific files by name rather than using "git add -A" or "git add .", which can accidentally include sensitive files (.env, credentials) or large binaries\n'[39m +

            [32m'\n'[39m +

            [32m'  - Run a git status command to see all untracked files. IMPORTANT: Never use the -uall flag as it can cause memory issues on large repos.\n'[39m +

            [32m'  - Do not commit files that likely contain secrets (.env, credentials.json, etc). Warn the user if they specifically request to commit those files\n'[39m +
